# Build execution permit lifetime

Collection: 2026-11-18T14:25:47.530470Z. Analysis source revision: `8d6408ac1f6f84afab72822fa190cd3d9e4a270e`. Goal: `8c48b9ec5a3a40c5b32c6beffffe0b2d`.

## Finding

**Fact:** The supplied recent cohort contains 2,903 attempts and 2,332 completed, paired execution-lock episodes. Their recorded held time totals **260,471,031 ms** (72.3531 permit-hours). Gate episodes have a median of **172,161.5 ms**; focused episodes have a median of **69,419 ms**. These are conditional on a retained execution-release record, including unsuccessful attempts, rather than estimates for all attempts.

**Unknown:** Compilation time, actual focused-test execution time and structural coverage hold time cannot be quantitatively separated for these episodes from this projection. Lease events have no UTC endpoint timestamps; check envelopes do not distinguish compilation, permit waiting and child execution; no hold registration/end events are supplied. All recorded heldMs therefore remains quantitatively unattributed. This does not mean those phases took zero time.

**Inference:** No execution-lock release change is justified by this measurement. Long holds and check-envelope overlap establish neither unnecessary ownership nor a cause of another attempt's timeout. Keep the existing release, artifact custody and coverage safeguards. No capacity, build-scope, runtime or instrumentation change is proposed.

## Measurement and attribution method

**Fact:** [ConductorParallelAcceptanceAttempts.cs](../../src/Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.App/Orchestration/ConductorParallelAcceptanceAttempts.cs), `AcquireAttemptStableSlotLease` at lines 1801–1818, starts monotonic timing after successful acquisition and registers a release observer. The emitted receipt at lines 1926–1937 has `releaseKind=execution-lock` and `heldMs`, but no UTC timestamp. Acquisition waiting is outside that measurement. [DotnetBuildEnvironmentManager.cs](../../src/Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.Infrastructure/Workspaces/DotnetBuildEnvironmentManager.cs), `ReleaseExecutionLockCore` at lines 2051–2089, disposes the stream, performs release bookkeeping, then invokes the observer once. Thus heldMs is the recorded observer-based duration, not a separately timestamped physical-unlock interval.

Pair each acquisition with the next execution release for the same attempt, permit and holder PID in retained event order. Preserve every episode if multiple exist; retain unmatched acquisitions and releases separately. No acquisition/release events were deduplicated by this analysis. All 2,332 releases in this input pair unambiguously with one preceding acquisition. Per-attempt totals sum only completed recorded episodes; no first-to-last span is substituted. The supplied cohort happens to contain one recorded episode per acquired attempt; this is not a universal lifecycle claim.

**Fact:** Current metadata persistence deduplicates identical receipt strings (`PersistLeaseReceipt`, lines 1978–1982). [ConductorAcceptanceLeaseReceipts.cs](../../src/Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.App/Orchestration/ConductorAcceptanceLeaseReceipts.cs), `Replay`, forwards retained receipts; [ConductEventLogWriter.cs](../../src/Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.App/Orchestration/ConductEventLogWriter.cs), `Serialize` at line 172, defaults an absent event timestamp to append time. Neither replay time, attempt start/end, nor a later custody release establishes an execution-lock endpoint. Later check-level permits can be acquired by `AcquireCheckPermit` ([GoalAcceptanceVerifier.cs](../../src/Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.Infrastructure/Workspaces/GoalAcceptanceVerifier.cs), lines 2728–2760 and 2795–2798); they are not measured by assuming the original attempt observer covers them.

Each ledger episode has the following explicit defaults: acquisition UTC **unknown**; execution-release UTC **unknown**; independently measured elapsed time **unknown**; `elapsed − heldMs` discrepancy **unknown**; compile ms **unknown**; actual focused-test execution ms **unknown**; coverage-hold ms and activation **unknown**; execution arm **unknown**; environment **E1**. These defaults apply to every row, including episodes whose surrounding check timestamps are shown. For completed rows the entire recorded heldMs is unattributed; for incomplete rows duration and completed-episode total are **unknown**, never zero. Confidence is high in the transcribed duration/identity and limited to the operator projection; confidence in quantitative phase attribution is unknown.

The ledgers retain zero-based event indices, plus the nearest timestamped event before and after each release in retained order. These are context, not wall-clock bounds. Timestamped events run backward in retained order in **156 gate rows** and **0 focused rows**, so sorting all records by timestamp or treating append order as synchronized execution would invent precision. Check-level envelopes can include build work, waits, retries and result processing. No compiler process intervals or coverage-hold intervals can be intersected with known lock endpoints. No phase/hold durations are added, so overlapping work is not double-counted.

| Retained ordering evidence | Gate | Focused |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Execution release before every recorded check start | 2 | 1,249 |
| At least one check start precedes execution release | 340 | 741 |
| At least one check completion precedes execution release | 340 | 0 |

**Inference:** The 741 focused releases between a check start and its completion do not establish retention through actual test execution: the check envelope may include its build, and the managed MTP path explicitly requests release before launching its test host. For the other 1,249 focused episodes, retained order places release before all supplied check starts. Both observations concern the measured attempt permit only; neither rules out later permits, omitted events or coverage work. Gate check completions under a held episode also do not distinguish legitimate coverage/build ownership from unnecessary retention.

## Recorded durations and incomplete evidence

**Fact:** Quantiles below use the completed recorded episodes only; p90 and p95 are nearest-rank quantiles (`ceil(p*n)`, one-based). Medians average the two middle values when n is even. Totals are sums across episodes, not cohort wall time or utilization.

| heldMs statistic | Gate | Focused | Combined |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| n | 342 | 1,990 | 2,332 |
| Sum | 91,136,336 | 169,334,695 | 260,471,031 |
| Minimum | 7,673 | 7,949 | 7,673 |
| Median | 172,161.5 | 69,419 | 78,410.5 |
| p90 | 549,560 | 167,678 | 213,271 |
| p95 | 636,836 | 208,143 | 387,588 |
| Maximum | 881,614 | 1,354,984 | 1,354,984 |

**Fact:** The longest gate episode is `8f1d41e2-0-20261117190701791-24b0c6ad317f40da98730359b4640608`, build-1/PID 15256, 881,614 ms. The longest focused episode is `ff075c94-0-20261112200432601-144ba251b5e2426ebbf308d4e5368262`, build-0/PID 6720, 1,354,984 ms. Their individual ledger rows provide source and event references. Neither maximum establishes a defect.

**Fact:** The **571 attempts without a completed execution release** consist of 142 acquired/incomplete episodes, 355 explicit wait/yield acquisition failures and 74 eventless attempts. All 142 acquired/incomplete rows are gate attempts: 115 have recorded outcome Cancelled and 27 ProcessDied. Their terminal metadata is not proof of a released lock or a zero hold. The 355 wait/yield rows record BlockedBuildSlot without acquisition; heldMs is inapplicable. Of the 74 eventless rows, 13 record BlockedBuildSlot (1 gate, 12 focused), while 61 have other outcomes. Classify those 13 as outcome-only acquisition failures; classify the other 61 as unknown permit history, not failed acquisition by inference.

There are **428 wait events**: G 30 and F 398. They include 355 yield cases, 69 later completed acquisitions (G 10, F 59), and 4 acquired/incomplete gate cases. Wait durations are not present and are not added to heldMs. There are **16 completed execution releases without a final custody-release receipt** (G 15, F 1); their measured execution episodes remain completed while custody evidence is incomplete. Conversely a final custody release must never substitute for a missing execution release.

| Recorded outcome (numeric value retained in ledger) | Gate | Focused |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Passed (1) | 229 | 1,284 |
| Failed (2) | 94 | 480 |
| StaleCandidate (3) | 1 | 7 |
| ProcessDied (4) | 42 | 1 |
| Cancelled (6) | 126 | 0 |
| BlockedBuildSlot (7) | 17 | 605 |
| Faulted (12) | 17 | 0 |

Outcome names use `ConductorParallelAcceptanceAttemptOutcome` at the inspected source revision (lines 15–31). Numeric values are preserved; no historical build identity or new cause classification is inferred from those names. Notably, **254 focused attempts with BlockedBuildSlot do have a completed initial permit episode**; an attempt outcome is not proof that it never acquired a permit.

## Historical 22-minute case

**Fact:** H identifies `9dcc4ea6-0-20261020020046618-a4b811290d80467d960d3a6373215411`, kind pre-review-evidence, recorded outcome 2, candidate `019314d457519909ae2789a5ce0139febb0793d3`, `mainHeadSha=null`, start `2026-10-20T02:00:46.618526Z`, completion `2026-10-20T02:26:34.7934215Z`. Canonical metadata: `.orchestrator/pre-review-evidence-attempts/9dcc4ea64bae44b78f5df990771cdb01/9dcc4ea6-0-20261020020046618-a4b811290d80467d960d3a6373215411.attempt.json`.

H retains WAIT → ACQUIRE build-0/PID 64660 → HANDOFF → first Infrastructure focused-check start → **execution release heldMs=1,319,508** → first check completion → subsequent focused checks → final lease release. The execution-release receipt and final lease release are distinct. Acquisition/release UTC are absent in H. The owner observation supplied in the objective places acquisition at approximately `02:01:03Z` and continued ownership during focused tests at `02:19:34Z`; those are historical observations, not independently timestamped endpoints in H. Adding heldMs to the rounded acquisition observation would estimate release near `02:23:02.508Z`; that is an estimate, not a measured release timestamp or discrepancy check.

| Retained focused check envelope (UTC, 2026-10-20) | Start | Completion | Execution-release position |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Infrastructure, first truncated selector group | 02:04:55.0156984 | 02:23:30.9919524 | Between start and completion |
| Infrastructure, second group | 02:23:31.2505760 | 02:24:36.0272808 | Before start |
| Infrastructure, third group | 02:24:36.2564087 | 02:25:59.6095879 | Before start |
| Core | 02:25:59.8290546 | 02:26:01.5338651 | Before start |
| Infrastructure.Cli | 02:26:01.7978566 | 02:26:33.2031962 | Before start |
| Infrastructure.Execution | 02:26:33.3739701 | 02:26:34.7099238 | Before start |

The first check envelope lasts approximately **1,115,976.254 ms**. It cannot be labeled an equal amount of permit-held test execution: the actual child launch, compiler boundaries and coverage hold state are absent. The 1,319,508 ms is one completed recorded episode (and the completed-episode total), outside the recent cohort.

**Fact:** Versioned source was rechecked with `git show <revision>:<path>`, rather than the stale line numbers in the old observation. Candidate `019314d457519909ae2789a5ce0139febb0793d3` already has `RunManagedMtpExecutableCheckCoreAsync` calling `ReleaseStableSlotExecutionLockUnlessCoverageHoldActive` at line **2753** of `GoalAcceptanceVerifier.cs`. Its `StructuralCoveragePreparation.cs` already has `BeginStructuralCoverageLockHoldForBatch` (291), `RequestRelease` (333), `End` (344), the immediate/deferred release helper (357), and hold-ending paths (381, 429, 479). Historical main `49cdbebbc35a6cfa8094fd1e9a9314b20e15db24` is locally readable, but H does not bind that revision to the attempt or executing binary.

**Unknown — governing path/hold:** The retained trace does not distinguish a managed command that needs the permit during its build/check envelope from deferred early release under a registered coverage hold, or identify the actual executing host revision. A check label and candidate source reachability do not prove which branch ran. Resolving this requires the source owner's contemporaneous full command/child trace and hold registration/request/end evidence bound to this attempt, plus its executing-host binary identity. Those records are not supplied; their original retention status is unknown. The owner answer explicitly permits an unknown result when this distinction cannot be made. This report therefore does not assign the 22-minute hold to coverage or unnecessary retention.

## Separate historical build-budget comparison

**Fact (supplied historical observation, not a newly inspected raw receipt):** Attempt `b18c853d-0-20261019223322147-d2913cef298a40d48104327f2f21e414` reportedly launched `dotnet build Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.sln`, exhausted a 20-minute build budget after **1,200,686 ms**, and held build-0 for **1,370,243 ms**. It predates the recent interval. The arithmetic difference is **169,557 ms**, quantitatively unattributed; acquisition waiting must not be inferred as part of that heldMs. Raw release/phase records for this comparison are not supplied.

Current [GoalAcceptanceVerifier.DotnetBuildPhase.cs](../../src/Mcg.AgentOrchestrator.Infrastructure/Workspaces/GoalAcceptanceVerifier.DotnetBuildPhase.cs), `AcceptanceDotnetBuildPhase.Create` at lines 93–106, still prefers a selected solution and then a root solution when available. This source fact neither attributes the historical difference nor proves an unnecessarily held lock. Focused-build scope remains outside this measurement.

## Safeguard audit and disposition

| Requirement | Rechecked source behavior | Evidence limit / disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Execution release versus artifact custody | `ConductorParallelAcceptanceAttempts.cs:1681` releases artifact custody, then disposes the lease and emits final RELEASE; the execution observer can have run earlier. | Outer lease lifetime cannot prove execution-lock retention. |
| Managed MTP early release | `GoalAcceptanceVerifier.cs:2549` prepares artifacts and requests release before test-host launch at 2561. | A focused check start is not an actual test-child start. |
| Structural coverage hold | `StructuralCoveragePreparation.cs:291` registers only with the required factory, stable slot/lease, build phase, parallel MTP infrastructure batch and execution owner. `RequestRelease` defers until `End`; completion/failure/discard paths end it. | No contemporaneous hold activation is supplied; preserve this safeguard. |
| Rebuilds and later permits | `GoalAcceptanceVerifier.cs:2758` reuses a held stable lease or acquires a check permit; remediation can dispose/reacquire at 2795. | Initial episode telemetry does not prove later compile ownership is unnecessary. |
| Baseline/control isolation | `GoalAcceptanceVerifier.FocusedEvidenceExecution.cs:61`, 215 and 269 route control work; baseline execution at 417–435 creates a separate environment and passes the stable lease. | Do not infer one candidate episode covers every arm or relax artifact isolation. |
| Child lifetime | Managed MTP runs through the lane host at `GoalAcceptanceVerifier.cs:2561`; managed dotnet commands run at 2764 and can require permits. | Projection lacks child lifetime/command details needed to prove safe earlier release on a specific path. |

**Inference:** The proposal threshold is unmet. The measured evidence supports keeping execution-release behavior unchanged. Missing phase/host evidence remains a report limitation, not an established product defect. There is no demonstrated interval of unnecessary execution-lock ownership to act on.

## Per-attempt and per-episode ledgers

Each CSV block is part of this report. `ref` is G/F plus the **one-based input line**; `goal` and `attempt` recover the exact canonical metadata path as `<canonical source>/<goal>/<attempt>.attempt.json`. `episode` is one-based within an attempt; `-` means no observed acquisition. `lease_events` preserves **all** projected lease events in original order, encoded `zero-based-index:action:permit:holderPID[:heldMs]` with A=ACQUIRE, H=HANDOFF, P=execution PERMIT_RELEASE, D=final lease RELEASE, W=WAIT, Y=YIELD. It includes incomplete episodes and waiting without converting either into duration. The literal `null` in a lease-event holder field preserves JSON null and is distinct from the original string `unknown`. CSV cells preserve `|` in busy permit/PID lists.

`held_ms` is the episode's recorded duration; `total_ms` is the attempt's sum of completed recorded episodes, or `?` if none completed. `?` in held_ms means unknown for an acquired episode and inapplicable without one. `state` is complete, acquired-no-release, yield-no-acquisition, blocked-no-events, or unknown-no-events. `phase_order` counts check starts/completions preceding the release in retained order, or `?` without release. `before`/`after` are the nearest timestamped contextual events encoded `index:kind@UTC`, or `-`; they are not endpoint timestamps. Every row inherits the explicit unknown acquisition/release UTC, phase/hold duration, arm and discrepancy defaults above. Full check labels and non-lease events remain referenced by input line/event index, not silently reconstructed.

The input summaries' stdout counts cannot be assigned per row. Candidate/main source identities are available in each referenced projection record but do not identify the executing host. No row is removed because its outcome failed, interrupted, lacked stdout or lacked release evidence.
